))}\n    91→      </div>\n    92→      <div className=\"h-9 rounded-md bg-muted\" />\n    93→    </div>\n    94→  );\n    95→}\n    96→\n    97→export function ProfileForm({ locale }: const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n   103→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n   104→  const [error, setError] = useState('');\n   105→  const [success, setSuccess] =     sms: false,\n   126→    },\n   127→  });\n   128→\n   129→  useEffect(() => {\n   130→    async function fetchProfile() {\n   131→      try {\n   132→        setLoading(true);\n   133→\n   134→ (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';\n   208→    }\n   209→  };\n   210→\n   211→  const handleEmailChange = async () => {\n   212→    if (!newEmail || !emailPassword) {\n   213→      toast.error(t('emailPasswordRequired'));\n   214→      return;\n   215→    }\n   216→\n   217→    setEmailChanging(true);\n   218→    try {\n   219→      const response = await toast.error(data.error || t('emailChangeError'));\n   232→        }\n   233→        return;\n   234→      }\n   235→\n   236→      setEmailSent(true);\n   237→      setEmailPassword('');\n   238→    throw new Error('Failed to update profile');\n   268→      }\n   269→\n   270→      const data = await response.json();\n   271→      setProfile(data.profile);\n   272→      setSuccess(t('successMessage'));\n   273→\n   274→      const newLocale = data.profile.locale?.trim();\n   275→      const currentLocale = locale?.trim();\n   276→\n   277→      if (newLocale && email_verified, notification_preferences, deletion_requested_at')\n    30→      .eq('email', user.email)\n    31→      .single();\n    32→\n    33→    // If no profile row exists, create one automatically from auth user data\n    34→    if (profileError && profileError.code === 'PGRST116') {\n    35→      const { data: newProfile, error: insertError } = await supabaseAdmin\n    36→        .from('users')\n    37→        .insert({\n    38→          id: user.id,\n    39→          email: user.email,\n    40→          name: user.user_metadata?.full_name || user.email?.split('@')[0] || '',\n sms: false },\n    45→        })\n    46→        .select('id, email, name, avatar_url, locale, currency, phone, email_verified, notification_preferences, deletion_requested_at')\n    47→        .single();\n    48→\n    49→      if (insertError) {\n    50→        console.error('[GET /api/user/profile] Error creating profile:', insertError);\n    51→        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to create profile' }, { status: 500 });\n    52→      }\n    53→      profile = newProfile;\n    54→    } else if (profileError) {\n    55→      console.error('[GET /api/user/profile] Error fetching profile:', profileError);\n    56→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to fetch profile' }, { status: 500 });\n    57→    }\n    58→\n    59→    return NextResponse.json({ profile const body = await request.json();\n    86→    const { name, phone, locale, currency, avatar_url, notification_preferences } = body;\n    87→\n    88→    // Build update object with only provided fields\n    89→    const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {\n    90→      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n    91→    };\n    92→\n    93→    if (name !== undefined) updates.name = name;\n    94→    if (phone !== undefined) updates.phone = phone;\n    95→    if (locale !== undefined) updates.locale = locale;\n    96→    if (currency !== undefined) updates.currency = currency;\n    97→    if (avatar_url !== undefined) updates.avatar_url = avatar_url;\n    98→    if (notification_preferences !== undefined) updates.notification_preferences = notification_preferences;\n    99→\n   100→    // Update user profile\n   101→    const { data: profile, error: updateError } = await supabaseAdmin\n   102→      .from('users')\n   103→      .update(updates)\n   104→      .eq('email', {"parentUuid":"bdf48431-099c-47b2-9568-ad989692f0e8","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a05e15fc55c427779","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JAK7mJ3ji8jWu1uduVtPpM","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     2→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n     3→import { cookies } from 'next/headers'\n     4→import { changePasswordLimiter } from '@/lib/rate-limit'\n     5→\n     6→const supabaseUrl = process.env.SUPABASE_URL!\n     7→const supabaseAnonKey = process.env.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!\n     8→\n     9→export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n    10→  try {\n    11→    // Get session from cookies\n    12→    const cookieStore = await cookies()\n    13→    const accessToken = cookieStore.get('sb-access-token')?.value\n    14→    const refreshToken = cookieStore.get('sb-refresh-token')?.value\n    15→\n    16→    if (!accessToken || !refreshToken) {\n    17→      return NextResponse.json(\n    18→        { error: 'Unauthorized. Please log in.' },\n    19→        { status: 401 }\n    20→      )\n    21→    }\n    22→\n    23→    // Create Supabase client with session\n    24→    const supabase = createClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseAnonKey, {\n    25→      auth: {\n    26→        autoRefreshToken: true,\n    27→        persistSession: false,\n    28→      },\n    29→      global: {\n    30→        headers: {\n    31→          Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,\n    32→        },\n    33→      },\n    34→    })\n    35→\n    36→    // Set session\n    37→    const { data: { user }, error: sessionError } = await supabase.auth.setSession({\n    38→      access_token: accessToken,\n    39→      refresh_token: refreshToken,\n    40→    })\n    41→\n    42→    if (sessionError || !user) {\n    43→      return NextResponse.json(\n    44→        { error: 'Invalid session. Please log in again.' },\n    45→        { status: 401 }\n    46→      )\n    47→    }\n    48→\n    49→    // Rate limit check\n    50→    const { success } = changePasswordLimiter.check(`password:${user.id}`)\n    51→    if (!success) {\n    52→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Too many requests' }, { status: 429 })\n    53→    }\n    54→\n    55→    // Parse request body\n    56→    const body = await request.json()\n    57→    const { currentPassword, newPassword } = body\n    58→\n    59→    // Validate required fields\n    60→    if (!currentPassword || !newPassword) {\n    61→      return NextResponse.json(\n    62→        { error: 'Current password and new password are required' },\n    63→        { status: 400 }\n    64→      )\n    65→    }\n    66→\n    67→    // Validate new password strength\n    68→    if (newPassword.length < 8) {\n    69→      return NextResponse.json(\n    70→        { error: 'New password must be at least 8 characters long' },\n    71→        { status: 400 }\n    72→      )\n    73→    }\n    74→\n    75→    // Verify current password by attempting to sign in\n    76→    const { error: verifyError } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({\n    77→      email: user.email!,\n    78→      password: currentPassword,\n    79→    })\n    80→\n    81→    if (verifyError) {\n    82→      return NextResponse.json(\n    83→        { error: 'Current password is incorrect' },\n    84→        { status: 400 }\n    85→      )\n    86→    }\n    87→\n    88→    // Update password\n    89→    const { error: updateError } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({\n    90→      password: newPassword,\n    91→    })\n    92→\n    93→    if (updateError) {\n    94→      console.error('Password update error:', updateError)\n    95→      return NextResponse.json(\n    96→        { error: 'Failed to update password. Please try again.' },\n    97→        { status: 500 }\n    98→      )\n    99→    }\n   100→\n   101→    return NextResponse.json({\n   102→      success: true,\n   103→      message: 'Password changed successfully',\n   104→    })\n   105→  } catch (error) {\n   106→    console.error('Change password error:', error)\n   107→    return NextResponse.json(\n   108→      { error: 'An unexpected error occurred' },\n   109→      { status: 500 }\n   110→    )\n   111→  }\n   112→}\n   113→"}]},"uuid":"f5195ad2-67d5-4eda-a21a-feff875642fd","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:33:20.852Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bdf48431-099c-47b2-9568-ad989692f0e8"}

{"parentUuid":"a7b42fb6-b199-4ab9-882c-6192502de601","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a05e15fc55c427779","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Aw3eQ2DdWvGRsVdzb27h7g","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     2→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin'\n     3→import { Resend } from 'resend'\n     4→\n     5→const resend = process.env.RESEND_API_KEY ? new Resend(process.env.RESEND_API_KEY) : null\n     6→\n     7→/**\n     8→ * POST /api/profile/delete\n     9→ * GDPR-compliant account deletion with 30-day grace period\n    10→ *\n    11→ * Implements soft delete:\n    12→ * 1. Marks account with deletion_requested_at timestamp\n    13→ * 2. Sends confirmation email to user\n    14→ * 3. After 30 days, cron job will hard-delete the account\n    15→ *\n    16→ * User can cancel deletion within 30 days by logging in again\n    17→ */\n    18→export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n    19→  try {\n    20→    // Get user from session token\n    21→    const token = request.cookies.get('sb-access-token')?.value\n    22→\n    23→    if (!token) {\n    24→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })\n    25→    }\n    26→\n    27→    const { data: { user }, error: userError } = await supabaseAdmin.auth.getUser(token)\n    28→\n    29→    if (userError || !user) {\n    30→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })\n    31→    }\n    32→\n    33→    // Get confirmation flag from request body\n    34→    const { confirm } = await request.json()\n    35→\n    36→    if (!confirm) {\n    37→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Confirmation required' }, { status: 400 })\n    38→    }\n    39→\n    40→    // Get user details for email\n    41→    const { data: userData } = await supabaseAdmin\n    42→      .from('users')\n    43→      .select('email, name, locale')\n    44→      .eq('id', user.id)\n    45→      .single()\n    46→\n    47→    if (!userData) {\n    48→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'User not found' }, { status: 404 })\n    49→    }\n    50→\n    51→    // SOFT DELETE: Mark account for deletion (30-day grace period)\n    52→    const now = new Date().toISOString()\n    53→    const { error: updateError } = await supabaseAdmin\n    54→      .from('users')\n    55→      .update({\n    56→        deletion_requested_at: now,\n    57→        updated_at: now\n    58→      })\n    59→      .eq('id', user.id)\n    60→\n    61→    if (updateError) {\n    62→      console.error('Error marking user for deletion:', updateError)\n    63→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to delete account' }, { status: 500 })\n    64→    }\n    65→\n    66→    // Send confirmation email\n    67→    if (resend && userData.email) {\n    68→      const deletionDate = new Date()\n    69→      deletionDate.setDate(deletionDate.getDate() + 30)\n    70→      const formattedDate = deletionDate.toLocaleDateString(userData.locale || 'en', {\n    71→        year: 'numeric',\n    72→        month: 'long',\n    73→        day: 'numeric'\n    74→      })\n    75→\n    76→      try {\n    77→        await resend.emails.send({\n    78→          from: process.env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || 'SKAPARA <noreply@skapara.com>',\n    79→          to: userData.email,\n    80→          subject: 'Account Deletion Confirmation - 30 Day Grace Period',\n    81→          html: `\n    82→            <h2>Account Deletion Requested</h2>\n    83→            <p>Hi ${userData.name || 'there'},</p>\n    84→            <p>We've received your request to delete your account. Your account is now scheduled for permanent deletion.</p>\n    85→\n    86→            <h3>30-Day Grace Period</h3>\n    87→            <p>Your account will be <strong>permanently deleted on ${formattedDate}</strong>.</p>\n    88→\n    89→            <h3>Changed Your Mind?</h3>\n    90→            <p>If you log in before this date, your account deletion will be automatically cancelled and your account will remain active.</p>\n    91→\n    92→            <h3>What Happens Next</h3>\n    93→            <ul>\n    94→              <li>For the next 30 days, your account remains accessible by logging in</li>\n    95→              <li>Logging in will cancel the deletion request</li>\n    96→              <li>After 30 days, your account and all associated data will be permanently deleted</li>\n    97→              <li>This includes: personal information, order history, saved addresses, and preferences</li>\n    98→            </ul>\n    99→\n   100→            <p>If you did not request this deletion, please log in immediately to cancel it.</p>\n   101→\n   102→            <p>Best regards,<br>The POD Store Team</p>\n   103→          `\n   104→        })\n   105→      } catch (emailError) {\n   106→        console.error('Error sending deletion confirmation email:', emailError)\n   107→        // Continue - deletion is already marked, email is best-effort\n   108→      }\n   109→    }\n   110→\n   111→    // Sign out the user from current session\n   112→    await supabaseAdmin.auth.admin.signOut(token)\n   113→\n   114→    // Create response with cleared cookies\n   115→    const response = NextResponse.json({\n   116→      success: true,\n   117→      message: 'Account deletion requested. You have 30 days to cancel by logging in again.',\n   118→      deletionDate: new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString()\n   119→    })\n   120→\n   121→    // Clear all session cookies\n   122→    response.cookies.delete('sb-access-token')\n   123→    response.cookies.delete('sb-refresh-token')\n   124→\n   125→    return response\n   126→  } catch (err: any) {\n   127→    console.error('Error in account deletion:', err)\n   128→    return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 500 })\n   129→  }\n   130→}\n   131→"}]},"uuid":"ee571021-0406-4528-8fcd-58ed0c642242","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:33:24.889Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a7b42fb6-b199-4ab9-882c-6192502de601"}

Supabase sends a confirmation link to the new email.\n     4→ * Requires current password for security.\n     5→ */\n     6→\n     7→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     8→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n     9→import { cookies } from 'next/headers'\n    10→import { changeEmailLimiter } from '@/lib/rate-limit'\n    11→\n    12→const supabaseUrl = process.env.SUPABASE_URL!\n    13→const supabaseAnonKey = process.env.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!\n    14→\n    15→export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n    16→  try {\n    17→    // Get session from cookies\n    18→    const cookieStore = await cookies()\n    19→    const accessToken = cookieStore.get('sb-access-token')?.value\n    20→    const refreshToken = cookieStore.get('sb-refresh-token')?.value\n    21→\n    22→    if (!accessToken || !refreshToken) {\n    23→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })\n    24→    }\n    25→\n    26→    // Create Supabase client with session (same pattern as change-password)\n    27→    const supabase = createClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseAnonKey, {\n    28→      auth: {\n    29→        autoRefreshToken: true,\n    30→        persistSession: false,\n    31→      },\n    32→      global: {\n    33→        headers: {\n    34→          Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,\n    35→ refresh_token: refreshToken,\n    42→    })\n    43→\n    44→    if (sessionError || !user) {\n    45→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid session' }, { status: 401 })\n    46→    }\n = await fetch('/api/shipping-addresses');\n    39→      if (!response.ok) {\n    40→        throw new Error('Failed to fetch addresses');\n    41→      }\n    42→      const data = await response.json();\n    43→      setAddresses(data);\n    44→    } catch (error) {\n    45→      console.error('Error fetching addresses:', error);\n    46→      toast.error(t('errorFetchingAddresses'));\n    47→    } finally {\n    48→      setLoading(false);\n    49→    }\n    50→  };\n    51→\n    52→  const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {\n    53→    if (!confirm(t('confirmDeleteAddress'))) {\n    54→      return;\n    55→    }\n    56→\n    57→    try {\n    58→      const response = await fetch(`/api/shipping-addresses/${id}`, {\n    59→        method: 'DELETE',\n    60→      });\n    61→\n    62→      if (!response.ok) {\n    63→        throw new Error('Failed to delete address');\n    64→      }\n    65→\n    66→      toast.success(t('addressDeleted'));\n    67→      fetchAddresses();\n    68→    } catch (error) {\n    69→      console.error('Error deleting address:', error);\n    70→      toast.error(t('errorDeletingAddress'));\n    71→    }\n    72→  };\n    73→\n    74→  const handleSetDefault = async (id: string) => {\n    75→    const address = addresses.find((a) => a.id === id);\n    76→    if (!address) return;\n    77→\n    78→    try {\n    79→      const response = await fetch(`/api/shipping-addresses/${id}`, {\n    80→        method: 'PUT',\n    81→        headers: {\n    82→          'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n    83→        },\n    84→        body: JSON.stringify({\n    85→          ...address,\n    86→          is_default: true,\n    87→        }),\n    88→      });\n    89→\n    90→      if (!response.ok) {\n    91→        throw new Error('Failed to set default address');\n    92→      }\n    93→\n    94→      toast.success(t('defaultAddressSet'));\n    95→      fetchAddresses();\n    96→    } catch (error) {\n    97→      console.error('Error setting default address:', error);\n    98→      toast.error(t('errorSettingDefault'));\n    99→    }\n   100→  };\n   101→\n   102→  const handleFormSuccess = () => {\n   103→    setShowAddForm(false);\n   104→    setEditingAddress(null);\n   105→    fetchAddresses();\n   106→  };\n   107→\n   108→  if (loading) {\n   109→    return (\n   110→      <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n   111→        <h3 className=\"text-lg font-semibold\">{t('shippingAddresses')}</h3>\n   112→        <p className=\"text-muted-foreground\">{t('loading')}</p>\n   113→      </div>\n   114→    );\n   115→  }\n   116→\n   117→  if (showAddForm || editingAddress) {\n   118→    return (\n   119→      <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n   120→        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n   121→          <h3 className=\"text-lg font-semibold\">\n   122→            {editingAddress ? t('editAddress') : t('addNewAddress')}\n   123→          </h3>\n   124→          <Button\n   125→            variant=\"outline\"\n   126→            size=\"sm\"\n   127→            onClick={() => {\n   128→              setShowAddForm(false);\n   129→              setEditingAddress(null);\n   130→            }}\n   131→          >\n   132→            {t('cancel')}\n   133→          </Button>\n   134→        </div>\n   135→        <AddressForm\n   136→          address={editingAddress || undefined}\n     />\n   143→      </div>\n   144→    );\n   145→  }\n   146→\n   147→  return (\n   148→    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n   149→      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n );\n    57→}\n    58→\n    59→function getCardIcon(brand: string) {\n    60→  const brandLower = brand.toLowerCase();\n    61→\n    62→  // Return brand name as text icon\n    63→  const brandDisplay: Record<string, string> = {\n    64→    visa: 'VISA',\n    65→    mastercard: 'MC',\n    66→    amex: 'AMEX',\n    67→    discover: 'DISC',\n    68→  };\n    69→\n    70→  return brandDisplay[brandLower] || brand.toUpperCase().slice(0, 4);\n    71→}\n    72→\n    73→export function PaymentMethodsList() {\n    74→  const t = useTranslations('Profile');\n    75→\n    76→  const setPaymentMethods(data.paymentMethods || []);\n   100→      } catch (err) {\n   101→        console.error('Error fetching payment methods:', err);\n   102→ method');\n   121→      }\n   122→\n   123→      setPaymentMethods((prev) => prev.filter((pm) => pm.id !== pmId));\n   124→      toast.success(t('cardRemoved'));\n   125→    } catch (err) {\n   126→ 132→\n   133→  if (loading) {\n   134→    return (\n   135→      <Card>\n   136→        <CardHeader>\n   137→          <CardTitle className=\"text-xl md:text-2xl flex items-center gap-2\">\n   138→ <CardDescription>{t('paymentMethodsDescription')}</CardDescription>\n   142→        </CardHeader>\n   143→        <CardContent>\n   144→          <LoadingSkeleton />\n   145→        </CardContent>\n   146→      </Card>\n   147→    );\n   148→  }\n   149→\n   150→  return (\n   151→    <Card>\n   152→      <CardHeader>\n   153→        <CardTitle className=\"text-xl md:text-2xl flex items-center gap-2\">\n   154→          <CreditCard className=\"size-5\" />\n   155→          {t('paymentMethods')}\n   156→        </CardTitle>\n   157→        <CardDescription>{t('paymentMethodsDescription')}</CardDescription>\n   158→      </CardHeader>\n   159→      <CardContent>\n   160→        {error && (\n   161→          <div className=\"rounded-md (\n   167→          <div className=\"text-center py-8\">\n   168→            <CreditCard className=\"size-12 mx-auto text-muted-foreground mb-3\" />\n   169→            <p className=\"text-muted-foreground\">{t('noPaymentMethods')}</p>\n   170→            <Button variant=\"outline\" className=\"mt-4\" disabled>\n   171→              {t('addPaymentMethod')}\n   172→            </Button>\n   173→          </div>\n   174→        ) : (\n   175→          <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n   176→            {paymentMethods.map((pm) => (\n   177→              <div\n   178→                key={pm.id}\n   179→                className=\"flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4 rounded-md border border-border bg-card\"\n   180→              >\n   181→                <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n   182→                  <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center size-10 rounded bg-primary/10 text-primary text-xs font-bold\">\n   183→                    {pm.card ? getCardIcon(pm.card.brand) : 'CARD'}\n   184→                  </div>\n   185→                  <div>\n   186→                    <p className=\"font-medium\">\n   187→                      {pm.card\n   188→                        ? t('cardEndingIn', { last4: pm.card.last4 })\n   189→                        : 'Payment Method'}\n   190→                    </p>\n   191→                    {pm.card && (\n   192→                      <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n   193→                        {t('expiresOn', {\n   194→                          month: pm.card.exp_month.toString().padStart(2, '0'),\n   195→                          year: pm.card.exp_year,\n   196→                        })}\n   197→                      </p>\n   198→                    )}\n   199→                  </div>\n   200→                </div>\n   201→                <AlertDialog>\n   202→                  <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>\n   203→                    <Button\n   204→                      variant=\"outline\"\n   205→                      size=\"sm\"\n   206→                      disabled={removingId === pm.id}\n   207→                    >\n   208→                      {removingId === pm.id ? (\n   209→                        <Loader2 className=\"size-4 animate-spin\" />\n   210→                      ) : (\n   211→                        <Trash2 className=\"size-4\" />\n   212→                      )}\n   213→                      {t('removeCard')}\n   214→                    </Button>\n   215→                  </AlertDialogTrigger>\n   216→                  <AlertDialogContent>\n   217→                    <AlertDialogHeader>\n   218→                      <AlertDialogTitle>{t('removeCardConfirmTitle')}</AlertDialogTitle>\n   219→                      <AlertDialogDescription>\n   220→                        {t('removeCardConfirmDescription', { last4: pm.card?.last4 || '****' })}\n   221→                      </AlertDialogDescription>\n   222→                    </AlertDialogHeader>\n   223→                    <AlertDialogFooter>\n   224→                      <AlertDialogCancel>{t('cancel')}</AlertDialogCancel>\n   225→                      <AlertDialogAction\n   226→                        onClick={() => handleRemove(pm.id)}\n   227→                        className=\"bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90\"\n   228→                      >\n   229→                        {t('confirmRemoveCard')}\n   230→                      </AlertDialogAction>\n   231→                    </AlertDialogFooter>\n   232→                  </AlertDialogContent>\n   233→                </AlertDialog>\n   234→              </div>\n   235→            ))}\n   236→          </div>\n   237→        )}\n   238→      </CardContent>\n   239→    </Card>\n   240→  );\n   241→}\n   242→"}]},"uuid":"7c512e04-6401-472c-99a9-9dabd293c155","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:33:28.240Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e0c26d4b-2f5f-4a96-b0bb-75d5142c1769"}

    1→'use client'\n     2→\n     3→import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'\n     4→import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'\n     5→import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'\n '@/components/ui/dialog'\n    15→import { toast } from 'sonner'\n    16→import { DeletionCountdownBanner } from './DeletionCountdownBanner'\n    17→\n    18→export function DeleteAccountSection() {\n    19→  const t = useTranslations('Profile')\n    20→  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false)\n    21→  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false)\n    22→  const [pendingDeletion, setPendingDeletion] = useState<string | null>(null)\n    23→\n    24→  useEffect(() => {\n    25→    async function fetchDeletionStatus() {\n    26→      try {\n    27→        const res = await fetch('/api/user/profile', { credentials: 'include' })\n    28→        if (!res.ok) return\n    29→        const data = await res.json()\n    30→        if (data.profile?.deletion_requested_at) {\n    31→          setPendingDeletion(data.profile.deletion_requested_at)\n    32→        }\n    33→      } catch {\n    34→        // Ignore — profile page handles auth redirect\n    35→      }\n    36→    }\n    37→    fetchDeletionStatus()\n    38→  }, [])\n    39→\n    40→  const handleDeleteAccount = async () => {\n    41→    setIsDeleting(true)\n    42→\n    43→    try {\n    44→      const response = await fetch('/api/profile/delete', {\n    45→        method: 'POST',\n    46→        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n    47→        body: JSON.stringify({ confirm: true }),\n    48→      })\n    49→\n    50→      if (!response.ok) {\n    51→        const error = await response.json()\n    52→        throw new Error(error.error || 'Failed to delete account')\n    53→      }\n    54→\n    55→      toast.success(t('accountDeletedSuccess'))\n    56→\n    57→      // Close dialog and redirect to home after a short delay\n    58→      setIsOpen(false)\n    59→      setTimeout(() => {\n    60→        window.location.href = '/en'\n    61→      }, 1500)\n    62→    } catch (err: any) {\n    63→      console.error('Error deleting account:', err)\n    64→      toast.error(err.message || t('accountDeleteError'))\n    65→    } finally {\n    66→      setIsDeleting(false)\n    67→    }\n    68→  }\n    69→\n    70→  // If deletion is pending, show countdown banner instead of delete button\n    71→  if (pendingDeletion) {\n    72→    return (\n    73→      <DeletionCountdownBanner\n    74→        deletionRequestedAt={pendingDeletion}\n    75→        onCancelled={() => setPendingDeletion(null)}\n    76→      />\n    77→    )\n    78→  }\n    79→\n    80→  return (\n    81→    <div className=\"rounded-lg border border-destructive/20 bg-destructive/5 p-6\">\n    82→      <h3 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-destructive mb-2\">\n    83→        {t('dangerZone')}\n    84→      </h3>\n    85→      <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground mb-4\">\n    86→        {t('dangerZoneDescription')}\n    87→      </p>\n    88→\n    89→      <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={setIsOpen}>\n    90→        <DialogTrigger asChild>\n    91→          <Button variant=\"destructive\">\n    92→            {t('deleteAccount')}\n    93→          </Button>\n    94→        </DialogTrigger>\n    95→        <DialogContent>\n    96→          <DialogHeader>\n    97→            <DialogTitle>{t('deleteAccountConfirmTitle')}</DialogTitle>\n    98→            <DialogDescription>\n    99→              {t('deleteAccountConfirmDescription')}\n   100→            </DialogDescription>\n   101→          </DialogHeader>\n   102→          <div className=\"space-y-2 py-4\">\n   103→            <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n   104→              {t('deleteAccountWarning1')}\n   105→            </p>\n   106→            <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n   107→              {t('deleteAccountWarning2')}\n   108→            </p>\n   109→          </div>\n   110→          <DialogFooter>\n   111→            <Button\n   112→              variant=\"outline\"\n   113→              onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}\n   114→              disabled={isDeleting}\n   115→            >\n   116→              {t('cancel')}\n   117→            </Button>\n   118→            <Button\n   119→              variant=\"destructive\"\n   120→              onClick={handleDeleteAccount}\n   121→              disabled={isDeleting}\n   122→            >\n   123→ )\n   130→}\n   131→"}]},"uuid":"5a9235a8-8a8e-4db0-af97-dc59dc0d1541","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:33:30.678Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"60b437a7-423c-40d5-8291-555555e392c0"}

designs_per_month: number\n    20→    mockups_per_month: number\n    21→  }\n    22→}\n    23→\n    24→export function PlanCard() {\n    25→  const t = useTranslations('Profile')\n    26→  const params = useParams()\n    27→  const locale = (params.locale as string) || 'en'\n    28→  const [usage, setUsage] = useState<UsageData | null>(null)\n    29→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)\n    30→  const [portalLoading, setPortalLoading] = useState(false)\n    31→\n    32→  useEffect(() => {\n    33→    async function fetchUsage() {\n    34→      try {\n    35→        const res = await fetch('/api/subscription/usage')\n    36→        if (res.ok) {\n    37→          const data = await res.json()\n    38→          setUsage(data)\n    39→        }\n    40→      } catch (error) {\n    41→        console.error('Failed to fetch plan usage:', error)\n    42→      } finally {\n    43→        setLoading(false)\n    44→      }\n    45→    }\n    46→    fetchUsage()\n    47→  }, [])\n    48→\n    49→  async function handleManageSubscription() {\n    50→    setPortalLoading(true)\n    51→    try {\n    52→      const res = await fetch('/api/subscription/portal', {\n    53→        method: 'POST',\n    54→        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n    55→        body: JSON.stringify({ returnUrl: window.location.href }),\n    56→      })\n    57→      if (res.ok) {\n    58→        const { url } = await res.json()\n    59→        window.location.href = url\n    60→      } else {\n    61→        console.error('Failed to open subscription portal')\n    62→      }\n    63→    } catch (error) {\n    64→      console.error('Portal error:', error)\n    65→    } finally {\n    66→      setPortalLoading(false)\n    67→    }\n    68→  }\n    69→\n    70→  if (loading) {\n    71→    return (\n    72→      <Card>\n    73→        <CardContent className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-8\">\n    74→          <Loader2 className=\"h-5 w-5 animate-spin text-muted-foreground\" />\n    75→          <span className=\"ml-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground\">{t('loadingPlan')}</span>\n    76→        </CardContent>\n    77→      </Card>\n    78→    )\n    79→  }\n    80→\n    81→  if (!usage) return null\n    82→\n    83→  const isPremium = usage.tier === 'premium'\n    84→  const isActive = usage.subscription_status === 'active'\n    85→\n    86→  return (\n    87→    <Card>\n    88→      <CardHeader>\n    89→        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n    90→          <div>\n    91→            <CardTitle className=\"text-lg flex items-center gap-2\">\n    92→              {isPremium ? (\n    93→                <Crown className=\"h-5 w-5 text-primary\" />\n    94→              ) : (\n    95→                <Zap className=\"h-5 w-5 text-muted-foreground\" />\n    96→              )}\n    97→              {t('planTitle')}\n    98→            </CardTitle>\n    99→            <CardDescription>{t('planDescription')}</CardDescription>\n   100→          </div>\n   101→          <Badge variant={isPremium && isActive ? 'default' : 'secondary'}>\n   102→            {isPremium ? t('premiumPlan') : t('freePlan')}\n   103→          </Badge>\n   104→        </div>\n   105→      </CardHeader>\n   106→      <CardContent className=\"space-y-4\">\n   107→        {/* Plan limits */}\n   108→        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3\">\n   109→          <div className=\"rounded-lg bg-muted/50 p-3 text-center\">\n   110→            <div className=\"text-2xl font-bold\">{usage.limits.chats_per_day}</div>\n   111→            <div className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1\">{t('chatsPerDay')}</div>\n   112→          </div>\n   113→          <div className=\"rounded-lg bg-muted/50 p-3 text-center\">\n   114→            <div className=\"text-2xl font-bold\">{usage.limits.designs_per_month}</div>\n   115→            <div className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1\">{t('designsPerMonth')}</div>\n   116→          </div>\n   117→          <div className=\"rounded-lg bg-muted/50 p-3 text-center\">\n   118→            <div className=\"text-2xl font-bold\">{usage.limits.mockups_per_month}</div>\n   119→            <div className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1\">{t('mockupsPerMonth')}</div>\n   120→          </div>\n   121→        </div>\n   122→\n   123→        {/* Credit balance (premium only) */}\n   124→        {isPremium && (\n   125→          <>\n   126→            <Separator />\n   127→            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n   128→              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n   129→                <CreditCard className=\"h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground\" />\n   130→                <span className=\"text-sm font-medium\">{t('creditBalance')}</span>\n   131→              </div>\n   132→              <span className=\"text-sm font-bold\">\n   133→                {t('credits', { count: usage.credit_balance })}\n   134→              </span>\n   135→            </div>\n   136→          </>\n   137→        )}\n   138→\n   139→        <Separator />\n   140→\n   141→        {/* Action buttons */}\n   142→        <div className=\"flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2\">\n   143→          {isPremium ? (\n   144→            <Button\n   145→              variant=\"outline\"\n   146→              className=\"flex-1\"\n   147→              onClick={handleManageSubscription}\n   148→              disabled={portalLoading}\n   149→            >\n   150→              {portalLoading ? (\n   151→                <>\n   152→                  <Loader2 className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin\" />\n   153→                  {t('openingPortal')}\n   154→                </>\n   155→              ) : (\n   156→                t('manageSubscription')\n   157→              )}\n   158→            </Button>\n   159→          ) : (\n   160→            <Button asChild className=\"flex-1\">\n   161→              <Link href={`/${locale}/pricing`}>\n   162→                <Crown className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" />\n   163→                {t('upgradeToPremium')}\n   164→              </Link>\n   165→            </Button>\n   166→          )}\n   167→        </div>\n   168→      </CardContent>\n   169→    </Card>\n   170→  )\n   171→}\n   172→"}]},"uuid":"ef28d8fb-cb38-4921-be3b-02796ea0ed20","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:33:31.071Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"97a89b67-68df-403a-abb6-5859cd07a880"}

    1→'use client';\n     2→\n     3→import { useState } from 'react';\n     4→import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl';\n     5→import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';\n     6→import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';\n     7→import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';\n     8→import { Checkbox } from '@/components/ui/checkbox';\n     9→import { toast } from 'sonner';\n    10→\n    11→interface ShippingAddress {\n    12→  id: string;\n    13→  label?: string;\n    14→  full_name?: string;\n    15→  street_line1: string;\n    16→  street_line2?: string;\n    17→  city: string;\n    18→  state?: string;\n    19→  postal_code: string;\n    20→  country_code: string;\n    21→  phone?: string;\n    22→  is_default: boolean;\n    23→}\n    24→\n    25→interface AddressFormProps {\n    26→  address?: ShippingAddress;\n    27→  onSuccess: () => void;\n    28→  onCancel: () => void;\n    29→}\n    30→\n    31→export function AddressForm({ address, onSuccess, onCancel }: AddressFormProps) {\n    32→  const t = useTranslations('Profile');\n    33→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n    34→  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({\n    35→    label: address?.label || '',\n    36→    full_name: address?.full_name || '',\n    37→    street_line1: address?.street_line1 || '',\n    38→    street_line2: address?.street_line2 || '',\n    39→    city: address?.city || '',\n    40→    state: address?.state || '',\n    41→    postal_code: address?.postal_code || '',\n    42→    country_code: address?.country_code || '',\n    43→    phone: address?.phone || '',\n    44→    is_default: address?.is_default || false,\n    45→  });\n    46→\n    47→  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n    48→    e.preventDefault();\n    49→    setLoading(true);\n    50→\n    51→    try {\n    52→      const url = address\n    53→        ? `/api/shipping-addresses/${address.id}`\n    54→        : '/api/shipping-addresses';\n    55→      const method = address ? 'PUT' : 'POST';\n    56→\n    57→      const response = await fetch(url, {\n    58→        method,\n    59→        headers: {\n    60→          'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n    61→        },\n    62→        body: JSON.stringify(formData),\n    63→      });\n    64→\n    65→      if (!response.ok) {\n    66→        const errorData = await response.json();\n    67→        throw new Error(errorData.error || 'Failed to save address');\n    68→      }\n    69→\n    70→      toast.success(address ? t('addressUpdated') : t('addressAdded'));\n    71→      onSuccess();\n    72→    } catch (error) {\n    73→      console.error('Error saving address:', error);\n    74→      toast.error(\n    75→        error instanceof Error ? error.message : t('errorSavingAddress')\n    76→      );\n    77→    } finally {\n    78→      setLoading(false);\n    79→    }\n    80→  };\n    81→\n    82→  return (\n    83→    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"space-y-4\">\n    84→      <div className=\"grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2\">\n    85→        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n    86→          <Label htmlFor=\"label\">{t('addressLabel')}</Label>\n    87→          <Input\n    88→            id=\"label\"\n    89→            value={formData.label}\n    90→            onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, label: e.target.value })}\n    91→            placeholder={t('addressLabelPlaceholder')}\n    92→          />\n    93→        </div>\n    94→\n    95→        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n    96→          <Label htmlFor=\"full_name\">{t('fullName')}</Label>\n    97→          <Input\n    98→            id=\"full_name\"\n    99→            value={formData.full_name}\n   100→            onChange={(e) =>\n   101→              setFormData({ ...formData, full_name: e.target.value })\n   102→            }\n   103→            placeholder={t('fullNamePlaceholder')}\n   104→          />\n   105→        </div>\n   106→      </div>\n   107→\n   108→      <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   109→        <Label htmlFor=\"street_line1\">\n   110→          {t('streetAddress')} <span className=\"text-destructive\">*</span>\n   111→        </Label>\n   112→        <Input\n   113→          id=\"street_line1\"\n   114→          value={formData.street_line1}\n   115→          onChange={(e) =>\n   116→            setFormData({ ...formData, street_line1: e.target.value })\n   117→          }\n   118→          placeholder={t('streetAddressPlaceholder')}\n   119→          required\n   120→        />\n   121→      </div>\n   122→\n   123→      <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   124→        <Label htmlFor=\"street_line2\">{t('streetAddress2')}</Label>\n   125→        <Input\n   126→          id=\"street_line2\"\n   127→          value={formData.street_line2}\n   128→          onChange={(e) =>\n   129→            setFormData({ ...formData, street_line2: e.target.value })\n   130→          }\n   131→          placeholder={t('streetAddress2Placeholder')}\n   132→        />\n   133→      </div>\n   134→\n   135→      <div className=\"grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3\">\n   136→        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   137→          <Label htmlFor=\"city\">\n   138→            {t('city')} <span className=\"text-destructive\">*</span>\n   139→          </Label>\n   140→          <Input\n   141→            id=\"city\"\n   142→            value={formData.city}\n   143→            onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, city: e.target.value })}\n   144→            placeholder={t('cityPlaceholder')}\n   145→            required\n   146→          />\n   147→        </div>\n   148→\n   149→        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   150→          <Label htmlFor=\"state\">{t('state')}</Label>\n   151→          <Input\n   152→            id=\"state\"\n   153→            value={formData.state}\n   154→            onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, state: e.target.value })}\n   155→            placeholder={t('statePlaceholder')}\n   156→          />\n   157→        </div>\n   158→\n   159→        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   160→          <Label htmlFor=\"postal_code\">\n   161→            {t('postalCode')} <span className=\"text-destructive\">*</span>\n   162→          </Label>\n   163→          <Input\n   164→            id=\"postal_code\"\n   165→            value={formData.postal_code}\n   166→            onChange={(e) =>\n   167→              setFormData({ ...formData, postal_code: e.target.value })\n   168→            }\n   169→            placeholder={t('postalCodePlaceholder')}\n   170→            required\n   171→          />\n   172→        </div>\n   173→      </div>\n   174→\n   175→      <div className=\"grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2\">\n   176→        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n   177→          <Label htmlFor=\"country_code\">\n   178→            {t('countryCode')} <span className=\"text-destructive\">*</span>\n   179→          </Label>\n   180→          <Input\n   181→            id=\"country_code\"\n   182→            value={formData.country_code}\n   183→            onChange={(e) =>\n   184→              setFormData({ ...formData, country_code: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })\n   185→            }\n   186→            placeholder={t('countryCodePlaceholder')}\n } from 'lucide-react'\n     7→import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'\n     8→\n     9→interface DeletionCountdownBannerProps {\n    10→  deletionRequestedAt: string\n    11→  onCancelled: () => void\n    12→}\n    13→\n    14→export function DeletionCountdownBanner({\n    15→  deletionRequestedAt,\n    16→  onCancelled,\n    17→}: DeletionCountdownBannerProps) {\n    18→  const t = useTranslations('Profile')\n    19→  const [cancelling, setCancelling] = useState(false)\n    20→\n    21→  const requestedAt = new Date(deletionRequestedAt)\n    22→  const deleteAt = new Date(requestedAt.getTime() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)\n    23→  const now = new Date()\n    24→  const daysRemaining = Math.max(\n    25→    0,\n    26→    Math.ceil((deleteAt.getTime() - now.getTime()) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000))\n    27→  )\n    28→\n    29→  const handleCancel = async () => {\n    30→    setCancelling(true)\n    31→    try {\n    32→      const response = await toast.error(t('errorCancellingDeletion'))\n    45→    } finally {\n    46→      setCancelling(false)\n    47→    }\n    48→  }\n    49→\n    50→  return (\n    51→    <div className=\"rounded-lg border border-destructive bg-destructive/10 p-4 space-y-3\">\n    52→      <div className=\"flex items-start gap-3\">\n    53→        <AlertTriangle className=\"size-5 text-destructive shrink-0 mt-0.5\" />\n    54→        <div className=\"space-y-1\">\n    55→          <h4 className=\"font-semibold text-destructive\">\n    56→            {t('deletionCountdownTitle')}\n    57→          </h4>\n    58→          <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n    59→            {t('deletionCountdownDescription', { days: daysRemaining })}\n    60→          </p>\n    61→          <p className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n    62→            {t('deletionDate', {\n    63→              date: deleteAt.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {\n    64→                year: 'numeric',\n    65→                month: 'long',\n    66→                day: 'numeric',\n    67→              }),\n    68→            })}\n    69→          </p>\n    70→        </div>\n    71→      </div>\n    72→      <Button\n    73→        variant=\"outline\"\n    74→        size=\"sm\"\n    75→        onClick={handleCancel}\n    76→        disabled={cancelling}\n    77→      >\n    78→        {cancelling &&     1→/**\n     2→ * POST /api/profile/cancel-deletion\n     3→ * Cancel a pending account deletion (within 30-day grace period).\n     4→ */\n     5→\n     6→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     7→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin'\n     8→\n     9→export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n    10→  try {\n    11→    const token = req.cookies.get('sb-access-token')?.value\n    12→    if (!token) {\n    13→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })\n    14→    }\n    15→\n    16→    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabaseAdmin.auth.getUser(token)\n    17→    if (authError || !user) {\n    18→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })\n    19→    }\n    20→\n    21→    // Check that deletion is actually pending\n    22→    const { data: profile } = await supabaseAdmin\n    23→      .from('users')\n    24→      .select('deletion_requested_at')\n    25→      .eq('id', user.id)\n    26→      .single()\n    27→\n    28→    if (!profile?.deletion_requested_at) {\n    29→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'No pending deletion' }, { status: 400 })\n    30→    }\n    31→\n    32→    // Cancel deletion\n    33→    const { error: updateError } = await supabaseAdmin\n    34→      .from('users')\n    35→      .update({\n    36→        deletion_requested_at: null,\n    37→        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n    38→      })\n    39→      .eq('id', user.id)\n    40→\n    41→    if (updateError) {\n    42→      console.error('[cancel-deletion] Error:', updateError)\n    43→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to cancel deletion' }, { status: 500 })\n    44→    }\n    45→\n    46→    return NextResponse.json({ success: true })\n    47→  } catch (error) {\n    48→    console.error('[cancel-deletion] Error:', error)\n    49→    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'An unexpected error occurred' }, { status: 500 })\n    50→  }\n    51→}\n button (opens Stripe portal)\n   - Upgrade to Premium button (Free tier)\n\n#### API Routes for Profile Operations\n- **GET/PATCH /api/user/profile** — Fetch and update user profile (name, phone, change (Supabase sends confirmation)\n- **POST /api/profile/avatar** — Upload and store avatar image\n- **POST /api/profile/delete** — Initiate account deletion (soft delete with 30-day grace)\n- **POST /api/profile/cancel-deletion** — Cancel pending deletion within grace period\n- **GET/POST/DELETE /api/shipping-addresses** — Full CRUD for shipping addresses\n- **DELETE /api/shipping-addresses/[id]** — Remove specific address\n- **PUT /api/shipping-addresses/[id]** — Update specific address\n- **GET/DELETE /api/profile/payment-methods** — Fetch and remove payment methods\n- **DELETE /api/profile/payment-methods/[id]** — Remove specific payment method\n- **POST /api/subscription/usage** — Fetch usage data and limits\n- **POST /api/subscription/portal** — Open
